# Mapping review

Generated by `data/harmonized/build.py`. How every example reaches an archetype, what could not be pooled and why, and what needs a decision. To change a mapping, edit the `archetype` cell of that example in `records/source-example.csv`.

## Summary

- **491 archetypes**: 242 quantified, 9 named by a source without a number, 1 whose numbers could not be pooled (see *Not pooled*), 108 with no example yet.
- **2586 examples**: 1521 mapped, 1065 marked `(none)`, 0 not yet mapped.
- **6577 parameter rows**: 3259 pooled into the summaries; the rest are listed under *Not pooled* below.
- **Hierarchy snapshot**: 669 nodes, sha256 `6838baf0b7af1265`. 0 nodes added and 0 removed since the last build.

## Whole-plant costs for UKERC measures

A UKERC measure's cost is the cost of adding it to a plant. Where `records/reference/ukerc-host-baseline.csv` names the archetype's host step and UKERC states one baseline cost for it, the whole-plant cost (baseline + measure) is a derived row, and that row is what pools.

## Set aside: needs an efficiency factor

UKERC costs on energy-service archetypes. UKERC states them per tonne of the host plant's product, with the fuel used per tonne; its per-GJ figures are per GJ of fuel input. Per GJ of output needs the efficiency, which UKERC does not give, so they are out of the summaries until a factor is established. The worklist, with each example's fuel use per tonne, is `set-aside-efficiency.csv`.

None.
